# Skills · H · part 25

970 entries, hehe-industry-drivers to hello-world. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **hehe-industry-drivers**: 盒子原创行业研究 Skill 组合中的行业驱动力分析专项。用于解释行业收入、销量、价格、利润、渗透率、供给或结构变化为何发生：先拆解需要解释的结果，再识别并核查需求、供给、价格与成本、技术、渠道、政策制度等驱动，最后给出主要驱动、作用路径、限制与观察指标。用户提出“分析行业驱动力”“解释增长或下滑原因”“进行多因素归因”“什么在推动或抑制这个行业”等请求时使用。 由 `hehe-industry-researcher` 派发任务卡时按组合模式执行；用户直接提出上述驱动力问题时独立启动，自行确认范围、搜索并审核材料、分析和交付本专项结果。 ([hexiaofeier/hehe-industry-research-skill-pack](https://github.com/hexiaofeier/hehe-industry-research-skill-pack/tree/HEAD/skills/hehe-industry-drivers) · ★328)
- **hehe-industry-pain-points**: 盒子原创行业研究 Skill 组合中的行业痛点分析专项。辨析不同参与者在明确任务、流程或产业节点上反复、持续，或在关键时点承担的时间、成本、收入、结果、风险或责任损失，并核对现有替代为何仍未解决。用于“分析行业痛点”“行业瓶颈”“梳理行业发展卡点或障碍”“判断用户抱怨与行业瓶颈的关系”等问题。 由 `hehe-industry-researcher` 派发任务卡时按组合模式执行；用户直接提出上述痛点问题时独立启动，自行确认范围、搜索并审核材料、分析和交付本专项结果。 ([hexiaofeier/hehe-industry-research-skill-pack](https://github.com/hexiaofeier/hehe-industry-research-skill-pack/tree/HEAD/skills/hehe-industry-pain-points) · ★328)
- **hehe-industry-researcher**: 行研分析师，盒子原创行业与企业研究skill组合中的总路由入口，负责把研究开题、公开信息搜索、材料审核、行业或企业分析、Markdown 成稿及 HTML 报告交付整合成一条完整路线。支持四类任务：行业研究（规模、产业链、商业模式、竞争、PEST 外部环境、趋势、驱动力、痛点）、企业研究（业务、经营、财务、治理、估值、投资逻辑）、行业+企业研究，以及只回答其中一个或几个问题的单独专项研究。 当用户说“研究/分析/调研某行业”“做行业全景或行业报告”“研究某公司、企业或标的”“做企业商业尽调、融资或并购研究”，或明显需要多个专项共同完成时使用；用户明确要求本 Skill 统筹一个或几个专项时也可启动。用户直接点名某个专项 Skill 时，由该专项独立执行。 不设计或执行访谈、问卷，不分析一手用户样本；不用于新闻稿、宣传稿、公众号文章、简单名词解释、翻译或纯 PPT/Excel/图表排版；不提供个性化证券买卖、仓位或收益建议。新闻、用户评论等公开材料可作为研究材料，但不代表本 Skill 承担写作或用户研究任务。 ([hexiaofeier/hehe-industry-research-skill-pack](https://github.com/hexiaofeier/hehe-industry-research-skill-pack/tree/HEAD/skills/hehe-industry-researcher) · ★328)
- **hehe-investment-logic**: 盒子原创行业研究 Skill 组合中的投资逻辑专项。分析行业机会、企业、项目、上市证券或具体交易是否值得投资、是否实际可进入，以及应通过二级市场、一级融资、老股转让、控股并购、债权或混合工具等何种方式参与；同时说明优先方向或企业、资金去向、所得权利、回报来源、为什么现在、预期差、风险与退出条件。用于“做投资分析”“梳理投资逻辑”“判断赛道或公司投资价值”“比较投资方式”“筛选方向或企业”“分析融资、并购、战略投资、预期差或退出条件”等问题；不提供个性化买卖、仓位、目标价或收益承诺。 由 `hehe-industry-researcher` 派发任务卡时按组合模式执行；用户直接提出上述投资问题时独立启动。两条路径都先确认适用的研究场景、投资方式与目的，再按需搜索审核材料、分析和交付本专项结果；不把“投资分析”默认解释为二级市场分析。 ([hexiaofeier/hehe-industry-research-skill-pack](https://github.com/hexiaofeier/hehe-industry-research-skill-pack/tree/HEAD/skills/hehe-investment-logic) · ★328)
- **hehe-market-sizing**: 盒子原创行业研究 Skill 组合中的市场规模测算专项。按问题选择市场机会、交易经营、采购预算、财政经费、已有规模续算、宏观统计拆分、保有量更替或类比代理等方法，提供公式、参数、情景与敏感性分析，默认交付 Markdown 主报告和带公式的 Excel 计算附件。 由 hehe-industry-researcher 派发任务卡时按组合模式执行；用户直接要求“测算市场规模”“推算市场空间”或相关市场机会测算时独立启动，自行确认范围、搜索材料、分析、自审并保存成果。 ([hexiaofeier/hehe-industry-research-skill-pack](https://github.com/hexiaofeier/hehe-industry-research-skill-pack/tree/HEAD/skills/hehe-market-sizing) · ★328)
- **hehe-pest-analysis**: 盒子原创行业研究 Skill 组合中的 PEST 外部环境分析专项。以行业为研究对象，分析政治与政策、经济、社会和技术环境及其对行业和行业内企业的影响；可做完整 PEST，也可只分析一个或几个维度。企业研究需要判断外部环境时，分析目标企业所在行业。 由 hehe-industry-researcher 派发任务卡时按组合模式执行；用户直接要求“做 PEST 分析”“分析某行业的政策、经济、社会或技术环境”“判断外部环境对行业有什么影响”“做宏观环境分析”等问题时独立启动，自行确认范围、搜索材料、分析、自审并交付专项报告。 ([hexiaofeier/hehe-industry-research-skill-pack](https://github.com/hexiaofeier/hehe-industry-research-skill-pack/tree/HEAD/skills/hehe-pest-analysis) · ★328)
- **hehe-report-presentation**: 规划商业报告的图表、数据需求与图文布局，或将已有计划书、行业与企业研究、用户研究文稿制作成专业报告。用户问“这页用什么图”“规划本章图表”“这些数据怎么呈现”，或要求商业图表、16:9及9:16和A4翻页HTML、下拉HTML、PDF和可编辑PPTX时使用。支持只交图表方案，也可继续制作；保留完整论述并核对实际页面。 ([hexiaofeier/hehe-industry-research-skill-pack](https://github.com/hexiaofeier/hehe-industry-research-skill-pack/tree/HEAD/skills/hehe-report-presentation) · ★328)
- **hehe-report-writing**: 专业研究与咨询文稿写作、润色、审校及分页编排。将已有材料写成研究计划书、行业与企业报告、用户研究报告、综合报告、摘要或逐页文案，改善含糊用语、分析展开、商业严谨性和AI腔。适用于“润色报告”“把客户需求说清楚”“加强分析解读”“去AI味”“检查方案前后是否一致”“编排报告框架”“生成模块脚本或PPT逐页脚本”“将完整报告分页”等任务；承接已有研究与方案，不代替数据分析或视觉制作。 ([hexiaofeier/hehe-industry-research-skill-pack](https://github.com/hexiaofeier/hehe-industry-research-skill-pack/tree/HEAD/skills/hehe-report-writing) · ★328)
- **hehe-research-project-planning**: 为行业、企业、用户及相关社会研究制定可执行的项目方案，将客户需求转为方法设计、研究框架、工作安排、实际日历和费用。用户要求研究计划书、项目建议书、研究框架、研究执行方案、排期、报价或项目变更时启动；可完整策划，也可沿用已有方案只处理排期或报价。包含必要预研，具体问卷访谈工具、正式采集分析及最终研究报告另行开展。 ([hexiaofeier/hehe-research-project-planning](https://github.com/hexiaofeier/hehe-research-project-planning/tree/HEAD/skills/hehe-research-project-planning))
- **hehe-survey-analysis-workbook**: 检查、标记并分析问卷或其他结构化调研回收的 Excel、CSV、SAV 样本数据，保留全量记录，交付质量标记和异常说明，由用户决定纳入范围，再生成无图表的统计工作簿；完整分析另交带左侧目录的下拉式 HTML 简报。用户要求“清洗样本”“检查回收数据”“判断答卷是否有效”“跑问卷基础结果”“逐题分析”“做交叉分析”“做打分题分析报告”或计算列百分比、TOP2、均值、显著性、Kano、漏斗等时使用。支持只清洗、只统计和清洗后统计，按本次任务接续已有成果。 ([hexiaofeier/hehe-survey-analysis-workbook](https://github.com/hexiaofeier/hehe-survey-analysis-workbook/tree/HEAD/skills/hehe-survey-analysis-workbook) · ★1)
- **hehe-survey-question-design**: 基于研究目标、目标人群和已有材料设计或修改用户调研问卷。用于设计问卷、拆解研究问题与变量、编写甄别和主体题、规划跳题与配额、建立题目分析用途和字段字典、质检及预试问卷；默认交付 Markdown 建卷详稿、同内容 Word 阅读详稿及 Markdown 纯导入稿，其他平台按用户要求适配。不分析回收数据。 ([hexiaofeier/hehe-survey-question-design](https://github.com/hexiaofeier/hehe-survey-question-design/tree/HEAD/skills/hehe-survey-question-design) · ★3)
- **hehe-trend-analysis**: 盒子原创行业研究 Skill 组合中的趋势分析专项。判断行业、技术、需求或商业化变化所处阶段、驱动机制、核心变量、时间关系、经营后果与证伪条件。用于“分析行业趋势或未来走向”“判断技术扩散阶段”“研究供需、需求演变或景气变化”等问题。 由 `hehe-industry-researcher` 派发任务卡时按组合模式执行；用户直接提出上述趋势问题时独立启动，自行确认范围、搜索并审核材料、分析和交付本专项结果。 ([hexiaofeier/hehe-industry-research-skill-pack](https://github.com/hexiaofeier/hehe-industry-research-skill-pack/tree/HEAD/skills/hehe-trend-analysis) · ★328)
- **hehe-valuation-analysis**: 盒子原创行业研究 Skill 组合中的估值分析专项。选择适用的可比公司或交易、DCF/FCFF/FCFE、SOTP、资产、股利或里程碑情景方法，建立可复算的企业价值或股权价值区间、调整桥、敏感性和隐含预期。 用户直接要求“给上市或非上市企业估值”“为商业尽调、融资或并购测算价值”“判断交易对价、分部或早期项目估值”“做反向估值或估值合理性检查”等时独立启动；由 `hehe-industry-researcher` 派发任务卡时按组合模式执行。独立启动时自行确认范围、搜索并审核材料、分析和交付本专项结果。 ([hexiaofeier/hehe-industry-research-skill-pack](https://github.com/hexiaofeier/hehe-industry-research-skill-pack/tree/HEAD/skills/hehe-valuation-analysis) · ★328)

- **hei_ge_er**: 黑格尔最著名的方法论创新是其辩证法——矛盾不是需要消除的缺陷，而是发展过程的驱动力量。对立面通过"扬弃"（Aufhebung，既克服又保留）达到更高综合。 ([111pointer111/claude-marketplace](https://github.com/111pointer111/claude-marketplace/tree/HEAD/skills/historical-persona-distiller/output/hei_ge_er) · ★4)
- **heic-image-convert**: Convert HEIC/HEIF photos into optimized PNG and WebP assets for this project. Use when an image cannot be inspected or used in a browser because it is HEIC, when preparing reference photos for image generation, or when creating privacy-conscious web assets. Also use to make a PNG-backed SVG wrapper when an SVG container is required; do not claim that raster photos become true vector art. - **heic-to-jpeg-bundle**: Convert a folder of iPhone HEIC photos to JPEG and package them for sharing — a browsable thumbnail gallery plus an optional password-protected ZIP sized to fit a static host's per-file cap. Uses macOS sips (zero install). Use when someone can't open HEIC (Windows, appraisal/CRM software, older tools), when you need to hand a non-technical recipient a JPEG photo set, or when prepping images for a static-hosted gallery. TRIGGERS - heic to jpeg, convert heic, jpeg bundle, photo gallery zip, heic wont open, share photos as jpeg, password protected photo zip. - **heic-to-jpg**: Convert .HEIC images to sibling .jpg files in place, recursively. Use when the user asks to "convert HEIC to JPG", "make jpg copies of HEIC photos", or points at a directory of iPhone photos that downstream tools can't read. Originals are preserved by default; conversion is idempotent (skips files whose .jpg sibling already exists and is fresh). - **heic-to-png-cleanup**: Convert HEIC/HEIF image files to PNG using ImageMagick on Windows, verify each output, and delete the original HEIC/HEIF files only after successful conversion. - **heidarlegt-vidmot**: Hvernig á að byggja viðmót sem lýgur ekki — takkar sem segjast hafa gert eitthvað, listar sem raða vitlaust, og reitir sem sýnast vista. Þrjár reglur: lesa svarið áður en sagt er „vistað", ekki sýna takka sem getur ekkert gert, og raða eftir því sem hægt er að GERA en ekki eftir stærstu tölunni. Notaðu þegar á að bæta við vistun, stöðuskilaboðum, hnappi á mál, eða röðun á lista — og þegar Agnar segir „takkinn gerir ekki það sem hann segist gera", „ekkert gerðist", „af hverju er þetta efst", eða „hvað er staðan". Kveikjuorð: lygandi takki, vistun segir OK, röðun, forgangur, hvað er efst, hnapp… ([aggisigurds-dev/slokkvitaeki](https://github.com/aggisigurds-dev/slokkvitaeki/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/heidarlegt-vidmot))

- **hekouwang-claude-skill-doctor-skill**: 会勇禾口王的AI笔记 · Agent Skill（SKILL.md）体检器。检查一个 Claude/Agent Skill 是否 符合"按需加载的指令包，不是单文件巨石"的最佳实践——评 description 触发质量、SKILL.md 篇幅、渐进披露（references/ 拆分）、脚本外置、可移植性（无硬编码绝对路径）、安全（无硬编码 密钥、宿主元数据与多 Skill 发现冲突），给出评分卡 + 按优先级的修复建议，并可代为重构。触发：用户说「检查我的 skill / SKILL.md 体检 / 这个 skill 规范吗 / claude-skill-doctor / audit skill / lint SKILL.md / 我的 skill 太长了 / skill 拆分 / 看看我的 skill 合不合规 / skill 对齐官方规范」。 任何"评估/审查/优化某个 Agent Skill 质量或结构"的请求都应触发。 ([huiyonghkw/hekouwang-claude-skill-doctor-skill](https://github.com/huiyonghkw/hekouwang-claude-skill-doctor-skill/blob/HEAD/SKILL.md) · ★2)
- **hekouwang-env-doctor-skill**: 开发环境与磁盘体检：默认只读扫描（不读用户文档内容、不联网、不上报），按数据/缓存/残留三分法给出判定与官方清理建议。clean 子命令只跑各工具官方的缓存与旧版本清理命令（npm、yarn、pnpm、brew、pip、uv），逐项确认后执行；不删除版本管理器目录、不删模型权重与容器数据；被判为残留的版本管理器只在报告里给手动卸载步骤。附 scripts/run-all-doctors.sh：会调用 claude-md-doctor 与 skill-doctor，额外报告配置与技能分数，属附加能力。 ([huiyonghkw/hekouwang-env-doctor-skill](https://github.com/huiyonghkw/hekouwang-env-doctor-skill/blob/HEAD/SKILL.md))
- **hekouwang-harness-check-skill**: 当用户要求检查、体检、回归、验收、解释或诊断会勇禾口王内容工作流 Harness，或维护、移植、打包、开源这个 Harness 检查器 Skill 时使用。统一调用 verify.sh 及治理、Hook、安全门、Episode 等检查，分别报告本地、暂存区、CI 与真实宿主边界。具体 EP 的文章、实验选型和渠道交付预检由仓内 hekouwang-precheck 负责。 ([huiyonghkw/hekouwang-harness-check-skill](https://github.com/huiyonghkw/hekouwang-harness-check-skill/blob/HEAD/SKILL.md))
- **hekouwang-stock-data-reader-skill**: 会勇禾口王 · 个股公开数据速读 Skill。输入一个 A 股代码，自动用 akshare 拉取「资金流 / 龙虎榜 / 估值band / 财务三表 / 主营构成 / 北向 / 日K」等公开数据，算出固定指标体系 （价格轨迹·主力进出·席位结构·利润质量·利润结构·估值·风险线索），并产出「中立事后复盘」 形态的财经贴图 / 公众号长文 / 头条三件套（V2 米白或 V3 财经风）。内置金融合规护栏： 不荐股、不预测涨跌、不给买卖点，自动套风险提示条。 触发词：个股数据速读 / 扒公司公开数据 / 资金面复盘 / 出个股报告 / 3天一只 / stock-data-reader / 给我一只股的数据体检 / 用 akshare 拉某股数据。 ⚠️ 个股走公众号/头条为主；小红书仅限"中立复盘/算账/名词科普"形态且守合规护栏（不荐股/不预测/零站外导流），别发以资金流·席位为钩子的机构观感版。 ([huiyonghkw/hekouwang-stock-data-reader-skill](https://github.com/huiyonghkw/hekouwang-stock-data-reader-skill/blob/HEAD/SKILL.md) · ★1)
- **hekouwang-visual-dev-skill**: 维护会勇禾口王 Visual Core 的正式主题注册表、设计 Token、字体合同、完整画布背景、基础组件与 fit/shoot 运行时，并检查跨 Skill 所有权和注册表漂移。当用户要求调整共享视觉能力或核对 Visual Core、Content Master 与渠道 Skill 的契约时使用。五类实验台和当期选型归 Content Master，XHS 的 visual-plan.json 归 XHS Theme；本 Skill 不制作单期 EP、文章或渠道图片。 ([huiyonghkw/hekouwang-visual-dev-skill](https://github.com/huiyonghkw/hekouwang-visual-dev-skill/blob/HEAD/SKILL.md))
- **hekouwang-yandu-deck-skill**: 会勇禾口王的AI笔记 ·「演读 DECK」沉浸式演示产线与发布 Skill。把一篇文章/选题做成「一屏一镜、可翻页、能自动播放」的 keynote 演示版网页（默认米白引擎，国标系列走 V6 焰彩白；暖黑/焰彩黑为存量历史态），并自托管字体、发布到 Cloudflare Pages（hekouwang.pages.dev）。 当需要：① 把某篇文章/某期内容做成「演示版 / 演读 DECK / 翻页演示 / keynote 网页 / 沉浸式阅读页」；② 给「演读 DECK」站加一期演示、加一个系列、改首页；③ 给某个系列换主色/换肤（白↔黑 token-flip、切 V6 焰彩）；④ 发布/更新 hekouwang.pages.dev；⑤ 自托管字体、解决首屏字重跳变(FOUT)、子集化思源字体；⑥ 站点留言板（Cloudflare Pages Functions + D1）加装/排错 时使用。 触发词：演读DECK / 演读 deck / 演示版 / 翻页演示 / keynote 网页 / 沉浸式阅读 / 发布到 hekouwang / 加一期演示 / 演示版引擎 / deck换肤 / 焰彩deck / V6焰彩白 / token-flip / deck留言板。 ([huiyonghkw/hekouwang-yandu-deck-skill](https://github.com/huiyonghkw/hekouwang-yandu-deck-skill/blob/HEAD/SKILL.md))

- **held-cards**: Hold new backlog cards while the host is busy, and file them all later in one lane and one PR (operator handoff rule 21). Use when you want to file a card but the host is busy or the PR queue is growing ("hold this card", "add to the cards-to-file list", "note a card for later"), to check whether it is quiet enough to file ("can we file the held cards?", "is the host quiet?"), and to file the held list ("file the held cards", "flush cards-to-file"). NOT for a blocking problem (main red, a stuck PR, a daemon down): that is filed and fixed now via `file-item`. ([chalbert/web-everything](https://github.com/chalbert/web-everything/tree/HEAD/skills-src/held-cards) · 2 copies)

- **held-object-geometry**: Locate the bottom, entering end or working feature of a held object relative to the hand. Use when clearance, placement or tool contact depends on the load's actual pose. - **held-out**: Use when reporting how well anything fitted or tuned performs — a model, forecast, classifier, heuristic, prompt, or threshold. The score counts only on data the thing never saw, against a bar set before scoring, a naive baseline, and a placebo. Use when a score came from the data used to build it, or when something was iterated until the eval passed. ([mikestangdevs/craft-skills](https://github.com/mikestangdevs/craft-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/rigor/held-out) · ★4)
- **held-out-cell-type-ablation-benchmarking**: Use when evaluating whether a Symphony reference mapping flags query cell populations absent from the reference as novel, or when reproducing the ablation benchmark of per-cell/per-cluster mapping-confidence metrics (AUCs, Supplementary Fig. 9). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/held-out-cell-type-ablation-benchmarking))
- **held-out-cell-validation-model-selection**: Use when training a custom cTP-net model on paired scRNA-seq/CITE-seq data and you must pick optimal network weights via held-out cells rather than training loss. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/held-out-cell-validation-model-selection))
- **held-out-classification-performance-evaluation**: Use when a trained sequence-based classifier (e.g. BOM/XGBoost on motif counts) must be scored on a held-out test set of labeled CRE sequences, and per-class and aggregate performance metrics are needed for validation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/held-out-classification-performance-evaluation))
- **held-out-classification-performance-evaluation**: Use when a labeled categorical class label (e.g. UCI wine quality ratings) must be predicted on unseen samples and you need train/test-disjoint accuracy and F1 for a tuned pre-processing + feature-selection + estimator pipeline. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/held-out-classification-performance-evaluation))
- **held-out-condition-splits-evaluation**: Use when evaluating whether a multi-condition scRNA-seq model predicts held-out condition combinations (e.g. &lt;ctrl,severe&gt; cells) correctly, comparing against baselines like scGen and scPreGAN. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/held-out-condition-splits-evaluation))
- **held-out-donor-validation-design**: Use when validating that a reference-mapping pipeline (e.g., Symphony) can transfer annotations (such as surface proteins) to query cells lacking the modality, and you need a donor-level split so query donors are never seen during reference construction. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/held-out-donor-validation-design))
- **held-out-lipid-species-validation-splitting**: Use when judging whether a trained lipid MS2 annotation model learned transferable fragmentation patterns — i.e., testing on spectra of lipid species completely held out from training rather than re-scoring training or random validation spectra. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/held-out-lipid-species-validation-splitting))
- **held-out-prediction-correlation-analysis**: Use when evaluating crossmodal generation quality of scMM (or similar multimodal VAEs) on CITE-seq data: need to quantify how precisely surface protein abundance can be predicted from transcriptome observations in held-out test cells via correlation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/held-out-prediction-correlation-analysis))
- **held-out-prediction-error-evaluation**: Use when running VI-VS/CRT variable selection and you must choose an importance score function (e.g., xy_linear OLS vs. MLP with n_hidden capacity), or when comparing FDR-TPR trade-offs across importance score choices in semi-synthetic experiments. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics/tree/HEAD/collections/multi-omics/v1/leaves/held-out-prediction-error-evaluation))
- **held-out-test-loss**: Evaluates language model generalization and overfitting by measuring cross-entropy loss on a held-out test set. It probes how well the model retains predictive performance when trained on repeated or constrained data subsets. Use when the user has predictions and gold and needs to compute held-out test loss. ([qhjqhj00/research-skills-pool](https://github.com/qhjqhj00/research-skills-pool/tree/HEAD/skill-factory/output/held-out-test-loss) · ★3)
- **held-out-test-set-evaluation**: Use when trained phenotype classifiers must be assessed on genomes that were held out from model training, to confirm cross-validation accuracy estimates (e.g. Traitar's 85.3%/86.7% macroaccuracy) generalize. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/held-out-test-set-evaluation))
- **held-out-test-set-generalization-evaluation**: Use when a supervised classifier trained with 10-fold stratified CV on the TCGA 19,737-gene training matrix must be assessed for true generalization on unseen held-out TCGA test samples (e.g. confirming mLR's 85% test accuracy). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/held-out-test-set-generalization-evaluation))
- **held-out-test-set-performance-evaluation**: Use when replicating CITRUS's performance comparison: you have output_dataset_CITRUS.pkl with test-set predictions and need Spearman/MSE metrics versus the affinity regression baseline. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/held-out-test-set-performance-evaluation))
- **held-out-timepoint-benchmark-comparison**: Use when a trained trajectory model must be validated on held-out progenitor states and ranked against existing methods on clonal fate-bias prediction metrics. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/held-out-timepoint-benchmark-comparison))
